Method: 利用分子束外延（MBE）的原子级精度，对SiGe顶势垒进行梯度组分调制，以工程化其能带结构，从而减少SiGe-介质界面处的电荷积累态，并增强对Ge量子阱中空穴气的有效限域。随后制备霍尔器件与量子点器件进行验证。

Result: 组分调制SiGe/Ge量子阱异质结在霍尔器件中表现出增大的稳定栅压范围。由该异质结构制备的量子点实现极低电荷噪声：1 Hz下平均幅度0.46 μeV/√Hz，为硅上生长Ge量子阱的最低报道值；该稳定性在少空穴区持续存在，数小时内无可观测电压漂移。

Conclusion: 组分调制SiGe/Ge异质结凭借降低的电荷噪声与增强的能量稳定性，在构建高性能量子器件（如长相干时间自旋量子比特）方面具有重要应用潜力。

Abstract: Composition modulation is a powerful technique for designing materials with tailored properties, fueling the development of advanced semiconductor devices. In this work, we have implemented this technique into Ge quantum well heterostructures, offering a promising avenue to address the critical challenge of charge stability in spin qubit devices. Harnessing the atomic-scale precision of molecular beam epitaxy, we have engineered the band structure of the SiGe top barrier via graded composition modulation, thereby reducing charge accumulation states at the SiGe-dielectric interface and strengthening the effective confinement to the hole gases in the Ge quantum wells. The enhanced charge stability of composition-modulated SiGe/Ge quantum well heterostructures is confirmed in Hall devices, featuring an enlarged stable gate voltage range. We have further fabricated quantum dot devices from the composition-modulated SiGe/Ge quantum well heterostructures and observed remarkably low charge noise with an averaged amplitude of $0.46\,\mathrm{μeV}/\mathrm{\sqrt{Hz}}$ at $1\,\mathrm{Hz}$---the lowest reported value for Ge quantum wells grown on silicon. This exceptional charge stability of the quantum dots persists in the few-hole regime, with no observable voltage drift over $\sim$hours. With reduced charge noise and enhanced energy stability, composition-modulated SiGe/Ge heterostructures exhibit significant potential for applications in building high-performance quantum devices, including spin qubits with a long coherence time.

</details>


### [88] [Néel-Vector-Dependent Altermagnetic Spin Splitting in the One-Dimensional Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11547)
*Xin Chen,Duo Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在一维受限的完全补偿共线磁体中，非相对论自旋简并可与相对论自旋劈裂共存；以[110] Ta2TeSeO纳米带为例，易轴畴中非相对论能带简并而SOC产生显著自旋劈裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上交替磁性（altermagnetism）通过补偿共线磁体中的非相对论自旋劈裂能带来识别。但在低维（尤其是一维）受限下，这一判据可能失效：边界兼容的子格交换操作可能使唯一Bloch动量保持不变，迫使非相对论自旋向上与向下谱重合，从而掩盖其交替磁本质。因此需要重新审视一维受限体系中交替磁性的识别与功能表现。

Method: 基于Lieb格构造，沿对角方向施加一维受限，使投影的d波自旋劈裂被抵消，同时保留实空间子格交换图案。引入自旋轨道耦合（SOC），使自旋与晶格锁定，Néel矢量取向选择不同的磁线群，从而可能重新显现自旋劈裂能带。以完全补偿的[110] Ta2TeSeO纳米带为原型材料进行验证。

Result: 在继承的易轴畴（N平行[100]或[010]）中，体系表现出非相对论能带自旋简并以及可观的SOC自旋劈裂；对于足够宽且保持母体易轴序的纳米带，该劈裂是平衡态属性。易轴纳米带在有限理想电导下还支持约35%的右行模式自旋极化。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在一维完全补偿磁体中存在隐藏于常规反铁磁能带内的交替磁性功能；非相对论自旋简并并不意味着缺乏交替磁相关物理，SOC可在合适磁畴取向与受限条件下恢复自旋劈裂并带来自旋输运效应。

Abstract: Altermagnetism is normally identified by nonrelativistic spin-split bands in a compensated collinear magnet. Under one-dimensional confinement, however, this diagnostic can disappear: a boundary-compatible sublattice-exchange operation may leave the only Bloch momentum unchanged, forcing the nonrelativistic spin-up and spin-down spectra to coincide. We show that nonrelativistic spin degeneracy can coexist with relativistic spin splitting in a compensated one-dimensional magnet. In a Lieb-based construction, confinement along the diagonal cancels the projected $d$-wave spin splitting while preserving the real-space sublattice-exchange motif. Spin-orbit coupling (SOC) then locks spin to the lattice, so the Néel-vector orientation selects a magnetic line group that can reveal spin-split bands. Using fully compensated $[110]$ Ta$_2$TeSeO nanoribbons as a prototype, we find spin-degenerate nonrelativistic bands and sizable SOC splitting in the inherited easy-axis domain, $\mathbf N\parallel[100]$ or $[010]$. For sufficiently wide ribbons that retain the parent easy-axis order, this splitting is an equilibrium property. The easy-axis ribbon also supports a right-moving-mode spin polarization of about $35\%$ at finite ideal conductance. Our results establish one-dimensional fully compensated altermagnetic functionality hidden inside apparently conventional antiferromagnetic bands.

</details>


### [89] [Phase-Controlled Majorana Zero Modes in Altermagnetic Topological-Insulator Josephson Junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11633)
*Hao Dong,Xun-Jiang Luo,Xiao-Hong Pan,Xin Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在三维交替磁拓扑绝缘体约瑟夫森结中，利用晶面依赖的安德烈夫相移和相偏压调控拓扑超导，实现并空间控制马约拉纳零能模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何利用交替磁序与拓扑绝缘体表面态的耦合，通过相偏压这一可调参数实现拓扑超导相及马约拉纳零能模，并摆脱对狄拉克点精细调节的依赖。

Method: 构建两个常规s波超导体之间的三维交替磁拓扑绝缘体弱连接约瑟夫森结；分析d波交替磁序导致的表面狄拉克锥晶面依赖动量移动，以及安德烈夫反射中净动量产生的额外传播相位；计算晶面分辨的安德烈夫谱与相图。

Result: 晶面分辨的安德烈夫谱在不同相偏压下出现能隙闭合，形成承载MZM的拓扑超导区域；相偏压可调控MZM的空间局域位置；拓扑相变对化学势的适度变化仅弱敏感，无需精细调节到狄拉克点。

Conclusion: 交替磁拓扑绝缘体约瑟夫森结提供了一个通过超导相偏压实现并空间调控MZM的平台，且具有较好的化学势鲁棒性。

Abstract: We exploit facet-dependent Andreev phase shifts to control topological superconductivity with a phase bias in a three-dimensional altermagnetic topological-insulator Josephson junction. In the weak link between two conventional s-wave superconductors, the d-wave altermagnetic order produces facet-dependent momentum shifts of the surface Dirac cones. The resulting net momentum of the states involved in Andreev reflection generates additional propagation phases that differ between facets. Consequently, the facet-resolved Andreev spectra exhibit gap closings at distinct phase biases, giving rise to topological superconducting regimes that host Majorana zero modes(MZMs). We further show that tuning the phase bias controls the spatial localization of these modes. The associated topological superconducting transitions are only weakly affected by moderate variations in the chemical potential, obviating the need for fine-tuning to the Dirac point. Our results establish a platform for realizing and spatially controlling MZMs by tuning the superconducting phase bias in altermagnetic topological-insulator Josephson junctions.

</details>


### [90] [Bridging steady-state and time-domain descriptions of molecular electron transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11684)
*Thibaut Lacroix,Namgee Cho,Clemens Vittmann,James Lim,Susana F. Huelga,Martin B. Plenio*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文建立了有限尺寸波包透射率与能量分辨NEGF透射率之间的定量关系：前者等于后者在波包能量分布加权下的谱平均。基于此对应关系，作者构造了非高斯辅助波包，使其谱权重编码Landauer能量窗口，从而可由时间演化直接获得电流-电压特性，并推广至手性诱导自旋选择性的自旋-声子模型中的自旋分辨输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳态非平衡格林函数（NEGF）方法虽广泛用于研究电子从输入电极经分子体系到输出电极的透射，但无法直接获取电子的瞬态动力学以及分子内部自由度信息。近期采用的波包方法可弥补这一不足，用于研究手性分子中的对映体特异性电子输运，但波包透射率与NEGF透射率之间的定量联系尚不明确。

Method: 推导有限尺寸波包透射与能量分辨NEGF透射之间的定量关系，证明前者对应以后者为基础、以波包能量分布为权重的谱平均；利用该对应关系构造非高斯辅助波包，使其谱权重编码Landauer能量窗口；并通过时间传播求解电流-电压特性；进一步在描述手性诱导自旋选择性的自旋-声子模型中验证该对应关系。

Result: 波包透射率被严格证明为NEGF透射率的谱加权平均；所构造的非高斯辅助波包的谱权重可编码Landauer能量窗口，使电流-电压特性可直接从时间传播中获得；该对应关系成功推广至自旋-声子模型中的自旋分辨输运。

Conclusion: 波包方法与稳态NEGF方法之间存在严格的定量对应，可将瞬态波包动力学与Landauer输运框架统一起来，为直接由时间演化计算电流-电压特性及自旋分辨输运提供了理论工具。

Abstract: Electron transmission from an input electrode, through a molecular system, to an output electrode has been widely studied using the steady-state non-equilibrium Green's function (NEGF) method. Recently, the wave packet method, which provides access to the transient dynamics of electrons as well as internal molecular degrees of freedom, has been employed to investigate enantiospecific electron transport through chiral molecules. In this work, we derive the quantitative relation between the transmission of a finite-size wave packet and the energy-resolved NEGF transmission, showing that the former corresponds to a spectral average of the latter weighted by the wave packet energy distribution. Exploiting this correspondence, we construct non-Gaussian auxiliary wave packets whose spectral weight encodes the Landauer energy-window, allowing current-voltage characteristics to be obtained directly from time propagation. We further show that the correspondence extends to spin-resolved transport in a spin-phonon model of chirality-induced spin selectivity.

</details>


### [91] [Interplay of Kekulé order, flat bands and electron-phonon coupling in graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11711)
*Dominik Szczȩśniak*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 石墨烯中Kekulé序通过碳键差异化导致导带在M点变窄（近似线性），而VHS附近态密度和电声耦合近似二次增强，为空带工程和关联量子相调控提供理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验和理论均发现Kekulé序能产生扩展平带并增强电声耦合，但两者共存的微观机制尚不清楚，需要明确晶格畸变如何同时驱动这两种效应。

Method: 从晶格畸变出发，通过碳键差异及相应电子跃迁的变化构建模型，计算导带带宽、VHS处态密度、无量纲电声耦合与形变势随Kekulé序强度的演化关系。

Result: M点附近导带宽度随Kekulé序近似线性减小；VHS和无量纲电声耦合近似二次增强；电声耦合增强主要由VHS处态密度增加主导，有效形变势受畸变影响较弱，与已有理论和实验一致。

Conclusion: 可控Kekulé序为石墨烯中工程化关联量子相提供可能，并预示1/4填充下导带可能变浅及非绝热电声效应超越Migdal近似的相关性增强。

Abstract: The Kekulé order in graphene is known to give rise to extended flat bands near the van Hove singularity (VHS) and enhanced electron-phonon coupling. However, the microscopic mechanism underlying the coexistence of these effects remains unclear. Here, it is shown that they jointly emerge as a consequence of the lattice distortion manifested through the differentiation of carbon bonds and associated electronic hoppings. In this picture, the conduction band around the $M$ point decreases almost linearly in width with increasing Kekulé order, while the VHS and dimensionless electron-phonon coupling exhibit approximately quadratic upward responses. The resulting enhancement of the electron-phonon coupling is governed predominantly by the increasing density of states at the VHS, with the effective deformation potential remaining weakly affected by the distortion. As such, the obtained results are consistent with previous related theoretical and experimental studies, while also pointing toward a potential shallowing of the conduction band under the characteristic $1/4$ filling and an increasing relevance of nonadiabatic electron-phonon effects beyond the conventional Migdal approximation. Altogether, these findings highlight the potential of controlled Kekulé ordering for engineering emergent correlated quantum phases in graphene.

</details>


### [92] [Hilbert-space selected switch of helical edges in an artificial quantum Hall insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11732)
*Naijie Ren,Zhiren Xiong,Kaining Yang,Yanran Shi,Hanwen Wang,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Neng Wan,Xiaojun Jia,Jianpeng Liu,Zheng Vitto Han,Yaning Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过CrOCl与转角双层石墨烯间的界面电荷转移，将两层原本解耦的Dirac朗道能级阶梯错位，形成复合填充态：在电荷中性处的(+2,-2)态完全绝缘，而(+6,-6)、(+10,-10)态保留反向传播螺旋边缘通道并表现出显著非局域输运，可实现人工量子霍尔绝缘体与螺旋边缘模的波函数选择性调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子霍尔效应中的一维拓扑保护边缘通道是构建奇异量子电子系统的关键要素，但通过静电限制或对称性破缺来人工重构朗道能级拓扑在实验上极具挑战。

Method: 利用CrOCl与大角度转角双层石墨烯之间的界面电荷转移，使每层石墨烯中原本解耦的两套Dirac朗道能级阶梯产生偏移，从而构建出新的复合填充构型；并通过实验与理论相结合的方式检验所观测能隙态（gapped states）的相互作用本质。

Result: 在电荷中性处，仅涉及零级朗道能级的复合(+2,-2)态成为完全绝缘态，纵向电阻达到GΩ量级；而(+6,-6)与(+10,-10)等高阶复合零填充量子霍尔态仍保留反向传播的螺旋边缘通道，呈现显著的非局域输运，可达局域响应的50%。该开关行为被归因于朗道旋量希尔伯特空间：当填充由(+6,-6)降至(+2,-2)时，正交的N=±1轨道分量被移除，边缘兼容通道消失，体态与边界输运同时被打开能隙。

Conclusion: 电荷转移为工程化人工量子霍尔绝缘体提供了一条直接路径，为螺旋边缘模的波函数选择性调控开辟了可能性。

Abstract: Quantum Hall effects (QHE) host one-dimensional topologically-protected edge channels, which can serve as an essential ingredient in exotic quantum electronic systems. Yet the manual reconstruction of Landau-level topology, by electrostatic confinement or symmetry breaking, remains experimentally challenging. Here, we show that interfacial charge transfer in between CrOCl and large-angle twisted bilayer graphene offsets the two otherwise decoupled Dirac Landau-level ladders in each graphene layer, creating a new sequence of composite filling configurations. At charge neutrality, the composited $(+2,-2)$ state involves only the zeroth Landau levels and becomes fully insulating, with longitudinal resistance reaching the G$Ω$ regime. By contrast, higher composite zero-filling quantum Hall states, including $(+6,-6)$ and $(+10,-10)$, retain counter-propagating helical edge channels and exhibit pronounced non-local transport, reaching up to $50\%$ of the local response. We attribute such switching-behavior to the Landau-spinor Hilbert space -- as the filling is reduced from $(+6,-6)$ to $(+2,-2)$, the orthogonal $N=\pm1$ orbital components are removed, eliminating the edge-compatible channel and gapping both bulk and boundary transport. The interaction nature of the observed gapped sates was further examined both experimentally and theoretically. Our results suggest that charge transfer provides a direct route to engineer artificial quantum Hall insulators, opening possibilities for wavefunction-selective control of helical edge modes.

</details>


### [93] [Interacting Tomonaga-Lüttinger liquid with impurity for interaction constant $K = 1/2$: Thermopower investigation, entropy variation and heat capacity densities associated with thermoelectric particle transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11810)
*Abdellah Touati,Redoune Zamoum,Farhi Houssam Eddine*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究含局域杂质的相互作用参数 K=1/2 的 Tomonaga-Luttinger 液体，推导非线性温差电动势精确表达式及线性响应 Seebeck 系数，揭示其与熵/热容的 Mott 型关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究相互作用一维量子系统中热电输运与热力学性质之间的内在联系，尤其是含杂质 Tomonaga-Luttinger 液体在非线性区域的热电行为及其与熵、热容的关系。

Method: 采用玻色化与反费米化方法，对 K=1/2 的 Tomonaga-Luttinger 液体求解含局域杂质模型；推导非线性温差电动势的精确表达式并取线性响应得到 Seebeck 系数；利用 Kelvin 公式从 Seebeck 系数导出熵密度变化与载流子热容贡献；并将结果应用于分数量子霍尔量子点接触和耦合欧姆环境的单通道量子导体。

Result: 低温下 Seebeck 系数遵循由透射系数能量依赖决定的 Mott 型关系，且与每粒子熵密切相关；由 Kelvin 公式得到的熵密度变化与热容密度贡献在低温下均呈 Mott 型行为。

Conclusion: 在相互作用一维量子系统中，热电输运与热力学性质存在紧密联系，Seebeck 系数可作为探测熵与热容等热力学量的有效手段。

Abstract: We investigate thermoelectric and thermodynamic properties of a Tomonaga-Luttinger liquid with interaction parameter $K=1/2$ in the presence of a localized impurity. Using bosonization and refermionization, we derive an exact expression for the thermopower in the nonlinear regime and obtain the corresponding Seebeck coefficient in linear response. At low temperature, the Seebeck coefficient follows a Mott-like relation governed by the energy dependence of the transmission coefficient and is found to be closely related to the entropy per particle. This connection allows us to derive using Kelvin formula the thermopower contribution to the entropy density variation and the charge-carriers contribution to the heat capacity density. Both quantities exhibit Mott-like behavior in the low-temperature regime. We apply our results to a fractional quantum Hall quantum point contact and to a one-channel quantum conductor coupled to an Ohmic environment. Our results demonstrate the close connection between thermoelectric transport and thermodynamic properties in interacting one-dimensional quantum systems.

</details>

### [94] [Geometric Ginzburg-Landau theory of charge ordering and commensurability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10678)
*Aneesh Agarwal,Rutvij Gholap,Mohammad Saeed Bahramy,Robert-Jan Slager*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文揭示量子几何在电荷密度波（CDW）形成与可公度转变中的关键作用，提出基于有效Ginzburg-Landau理论的普适判据，并在过渡金属硫族化合物中验证，解决了纯动力学CDW判据失效的长期难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子几何概念近来在物理响应、超导、光学跃迁等领域带来新洞见，且效应在近平坦色散体系中最为显著。然而其在电荷密度波形成这一重要物理现象中的作用尚不明确；同时，既有纯动力学CDW判据无法正确描述过渡金属硫族化合物的有序波矢，这一长期谜题亟待解决。

Method: 作者推导了包含此前未被探索贡献的有效Ginzburg-Landau理论，并由此发现CDW形成与可公度转变的普适判据；在该判据中，底层电子-声子相互作用完全表现为电子量子几何的增强或抑制。随后将该理论框架应用于一类过渡金属硫族化合物进行基准验证。

Result: 研究发现量子几何在CDW形成中扮演核心角色；提出的判据能够同时刻画CDW形成与可公度转变；在一类过渡金属硫族化合物中，该框架成功描述了正确的有序波矢，解决了纯动力学CDW判据失效的长期难题，并给出稳健的判据与基础性洞见。

Conclusion: 该理论不仅与多个实验电荷有序体系直接相关，还可直接推广应用于超导等其它声子介导相，并可作为探索这些状态间相互作用的工具；更广泛而言，该框架为研究量子几何在相变中的作用提供了通用方法/范式。

Abstract: The concept of quantum geometry has recently led to reinvigorated insights in a wide range of fields including physical responses, superconductivity, and optical transitions, with effects most pronounced in systems with nearly flat dispersion. Here, we show that it plays an essential role in charge density wave formation (CDW) -- an important physical phenomenon that is responsible for driving various sharp changes in material transport properties including metal-insulator transitions. We derive an effective Ginzburg-Landau theory including uncharted contributions and, as a highlight, discover a general criterion for both CDW formation and commensurability transitions where underlying electron-phonon interactions manifest purely as electronic quantum geometric enhancements/suppressions. We benchmark our framework in a class of transition-metal dichalcogenides and resolve a longstanding puzzle where well-established purely kinetic CDW criteria fail in describing the correct ordering wavevector. Besides rendering robust criteria and fundamental insights that are immediately relevant to several experimental charge ordering systems, our theory can also be applied directly to other phonon-mediated phases such as superconductivity, and can be used as an important tool to explore the interplay between various such states. More generally, our framework provides a recipe for investigating the role of quantum geometry in phase transitions.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替磁性是一种净磁化强度为零却具有动量依赖自旋劈裂的共线磁相。已有研究多聚焦电子结构，对两类微观机制（非磁环境不等价、轨道有序）所稳定的交替磁性，其集体激发的统一理解仍然缺失。

Method: 在装饰正方格子上构建扩展的Kugel'-Khomskiĭ自旋-轨道模型，同时纳入非磁环境不等价与关联驱动的轨道有序；采用自洽平均场自旋波-轨道波形式，并结合互补的经典Monte Carlo模拟研究有限温度演化。

Result: 发现互不杂化但相互依赖的磁振子与轨道振子激发，呈现特征性手性劈裂；该劈裂源于两个不同微观贡献：非磁环境不等价导致的晶格依赖项，以及即使在等价非磁环境下仍存在的轨道有序诱导交换各向异性项。有限温度下自旋波与轨道波近似因虚假一级相变而失效，经典Monte Carlo则恢复预期的连续二级行为。

Conclusion: 本工作为理解交替磁体中磁振子与轨道振子的集体激发建立了统一的微观框架，统合了两类交替磁性的集体激发描述。

Abstract: Altermagnetism has recently emerged as a distinct collinear magnetic phase exhibiting momentum-dependent spin-splitting despite vanishing net magnetization, as a consequence of inequivalent non-magnetic environments. Lately, it has been proposed that strong electronic correlations may yield spontaneous altermagnetism due to orbital ordering even for equivalent non-magnetic environments. While previous studies have largely focused on the electronic structure, a unified understanding of the collective excitations associated with these two different microscopic mechanisms stabilizing altermagnetism remains absent. Here, we develop an extended Kugel'-Khomskiĭ spin-orbital model on a decorated square lattice that simultaneously incorporates inequivalent non-magnetic environments and correlation-driven orbital ordering within a common theoretical framework. Employing a self-consistent mean-field spin-wave orbital-wave formalism, we demonstrate the emergence of mutually unhybridized but interdependent magnon and orbiton excitations exhibiting characteristic chiral-splitting. We show that the splitting originates from two distinct microscopic contributions: a lattice-dependent term arising from inequivalent non-magnetic environments and an orbital-order-induced exchange-anisotropy term that survives even for equivalent non-magnetic environments. The proposed framework therefore unifies the collective excitations associated with both types of altermagnetism. We further investigate the finite-temperature evolution of the coupled spin-orbital system, revealing the breakdown of spin-wave and orbital-wave approximations through spurious 1st-order transitions, while complementary classical Monte Carlo simulations recover the expected continuous 2nd-order behaviour. Our work establishes a unified microscopic framework for understanding collective magnon and orbiton excitations in altermagnets.

</details>

*Lucas Z. Brito,Salvatore D. Pace*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文构造并研究了(2+1)维量子格点系统中有限2-群对称性的格点实现，涵盖分裂2-群与中心2-群两类，验证了其与量子场论预期的一致性，并给出了相应的局域对称哈密顿量及可解极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限2-群对称性在量子场论中已有丰富描述，但其在具有有限维张量积希尔伯特空间的格点系统中的显式实现尚不充分，需要构造格点层面的2-群对称算符以连接场论预期与格点模型。

Method: 针对0-形式对称群G与1-形式对称群A构成的两类2-群（Postnikov类[β]平凡的分裂2-群、作用ρ平凡的中心2-群），在全张量积希尔伯特空间上构造对称算符：其在A 1-形式对称的拓扑子空间上约化为2-群对称算符。通过考察带对称荷算符的变换、格点2-群对称缺陷结构，以及对格点2-群对称性做gauging得到的对偶融合2-范畴对称性，研究ρ与[β]的各种表现。

Result: 格点分裂2-群对称算符是onsite的，而格点中心2-群对称算符非onsite，需引入ancilla后方可变为onsite。构造的表现与量子场论预期一致。进一步提出两类格点2-群对称性的局域对称哈密顿量族，并识别出自发2-群对称破缺相与含非平凡对称富化拓扑序的可解极限；其中一种极限下，gauging后的哈密顿量是对应2-群规范理论的可解格点实现，并计算了其基态简并度。

Conclusion: 该工作为有限2-群对称性提供了系统的格点实现框架，明确了分裂与中心两类2-群在格点上的onsite性质差异，并通过缺陷结构、对偶融合2-范畴及可解哈密顿量极限，建立了格点模型与2-群量子场论之间的对应关系。

Abstract: We construct and study lattice realizations of finite 2-group symmetries in ${2+1}$d quantum lattice systems with finite-dimensional tensor-product Hilbert spaces. We focus on two broad classes of 2-groups with 0-form symmetry group $G$ and 1-form symmetry group $A$: split 2-groups with trivial Postnikov class ${[β]\in\mathcal{H}^3(G,A)}$, and central 2-groups with trivial action ${ρ\colon G\to\text{Aut}(A)}$. In both cases, we construct symmetry operators on the full tensor-product Hilbert space that become 2-group symmetry operators when restricted to the topological subspace of the lattice $A$ 1-form symmetry. While the lattice split 2-group symmetry operators are onsite, the lattice central 2-group symmetry operators are not, and can only be made onsite after introducing ancillae. We extensively explore various manifestations of $ρ$ and $[β]$ for these lattice 2-group symmetry operators and demonstrate their agreement with expectations from quantum field theory. These manifestations arise in the transformation of operators carrying symmetry charge, the structure of lattice 2-group symmetry defects, and the dual fusion 2-category symmetries obtained by gauging the lattice 2-group symmetries. We further propose families of local symmetric Hamiltonians for both classes of lattice 2-group symmetries and identify exactly solvable limits lying in phases with spontaneous 2-group symmetry breaking and nontrivial symmetry-enriched topological order. In one such limit, the gauged Hamiltonians are exactly solvable lattice realizations of the corresponding 2-group gauge theories, whose ground-state degeneracies we calculate.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构建强关联单轨道(d_{x²-y²})双层t-J⊥-V模型，其中V为库仑相互作用。采用受控大N展开方法，在平均场层次及其修正（beyond mean-field）上求解，重点考察面外自旋交换相互作用J⊥的效应。

Result: J⊥同时触发面外s波超导和面外键序相(z-BOP)不稳定性。z-BOP产生以虚部为主导的复数z轴跃迁，驱动面外电流并诱导面内电流，在垂直格板上自发形成破坏时间反演对称性的环路电流态。环路电流相与超导的竞争给出穹顶状超导区，最优超导位于z-BOP量子临界点附近。

Conclusion: 所得相图包含纯环路电流区、低掺杂共存相、较高掺杂的纯超导态以及关联金属态。该结果为双层镍酸盐中J⊥诱导的超导与时间反演对称性破缺态之间的竞争与共存提供了统一图像。

Abstract: The recent discovery of high-$T_c$ superconductivity in pressurized and thin-film bilayer nickelates, featuring a strong interlayer exchange coupling, and their potential similarities with cuprate superconductors, has made this a very active topic in condensed matter physics. In the present paper we study the strongly correlated one-orbital ($d_{x^2-y^2}$) bilayer $t$-$J_\perp$-$V$ model for nickelates, where $V$ denotes the Coulomb interactions, using a controlled large-$N$ expansion at and beyond the mean-field level. Focusing on the out-of-plane spin exchange interaction ($J_\perp$), we find that it triggers both out-of-plane $s$-wave superconductivity and an out-of-plane bond-order phase ($z$-BOP) instability. The $z$-BOP gives rise to a complex $z$-axis hopping dominated by its imaginary component, which drives out-of-plane currents and induces in-plane ones, spontaneously forming on the vertical plaquettes a loop-current state that breaks time-reversal symmetry. Competition between this loop-current phase and superconductivity yields a dome-shaped superconducting region, with optimal superconductivity occurring near the $z$-BOP quantum critical point. The resulting phase diagram features a pure loop-current region, a low-doping coexistence phase, a pure superconducting state at higher doping, and a correlated metallic state.

</details>

TL;DR: 在NdNiO₃光致绝缘体-金属转变中，金属性与结构对称性在非平衡条件下解耦，光激发可稳定一种兼具金属反射率与单斜对称性的亚稳"单斜金属"相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关联材料中电子序与结构序在非平衡条件下是否保持耦合，是一个核心科学问题，此前缺乏同时追踪金属性与对称性的实验手段。

Method: 以NdNiO₃为对象，结合时间分辨光学反射率（探测金属性）与对称性敏感的二次谐波产生（探测对称性），在光致绝缘体-金属转变过程中同步测量两种序参量随激发通量的演化。

Result: 金属性反射率在显著更低的激发通量下即出现，而正交高温对称性的恢复需要更高通量；因而在中间通量区间形成亚稳态，兼具金属相反射率与绝缘相单斜对称性，即光诱导的单斜金属。只有在更高通量下，对称性才完全恢复到高温相。

Conclusion: 结果证明金属性与结构对称性在非平衡下可以解耦，并确立同步多探针光谱学作为识别关联材料中新兴物相的有力手段。

Abstract: Understanding whether electronic and structural order remain coupled under nonequilibrium conditions is a central challenge in correlated materials. Here, we simultaneously track metallicity and symmetry across the photoinduced insulator-to-metal transition in NdNiO$_3$ using time-resolved optical reflectivity and symmetry-sensitive second-harmonic generation. We find that metallic reflectivity emerges at significantly lower excitation fluence than restoration of the orthorhombic high-temperature symmetry. As a result, optical excitation stabilizes a metastable state that combines the reflectivity of the metallic phase with the monoclinic symmetry of the insulating phase, revealing an optically induced monoclinic metal. Only at substantially higher fluences does the symmetry fully recover to that of the high-temperature phase. These results demonstrate a nonequilibrium decoupling of metallicity and structural symmetry and establish simultaneous multiprobe spectroscopy as a powerful approach for identifying emergent phases in correlated materials.

</details>


### [99] [Instabilities in self-consistent diagrammatic approaches and how to cure them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11405)
*Marcel Gievers,Herbert Eßl,Stefan Rohshap,Anna Kauch,Alessandro Toschi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文指出自洽图解方法中"误导性收敛"源于迭代格式的稳定性条件而非自洽图解描述本身失效，并提出通过反转雅可比矩阵不稳定特征方向来修正迭代过程的通用补救方案，在严格可解模型上验证其可将物理不动点稳定至强耦合区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自洽图解方法广泛用于计算关联量子材料性质，但在最富物理意义的参数区（中强电子相互作用）其适用性严重受限；迭代格式倾向于收敛到非物理不动点，即"误导性收敛"，且该问题与计算数值精度无关，亟需澄清其根源并给出通用解决办法。

Method: 在玻色子交换形式主义的统一图解框架下，对若干已建立的自洽多电子方法进行显式分析，判定问题根源在于各迭代格式的稳定性条件；随后采用反转自洽算法不动点对应雅可比矩阵不稳定特征方向的迭代重构方案（Phys. Rev. Lett. 137, 016502 (2026)），并在可严格求解的测试模型上开展系统性计算。

Result: 显式验证了"误导性收敛"源于迭代格式的稳定性条件，而非自洽图解描述的内在失效；反转雅可比不稳定特征方向后可稳定物理不动点，且该结论在所考察的图解方案中贯穿整个参数范围，包括最具挑战性的非微扰/强耦合区。

Conclusion: 根本问题在于迭代稳定性而非图解理论本身，故可用简单通用的迭代重构加以修复；所提方案能使自洽图解方案的物理不动点在原则上全参数区（含强耦合）稳定收敛，从而拓展其适用性。

Abstract: While self-consistent diagrammatic approaches are widely used to compute the physical properties of correlated quantum materials, their applicability may get severely hindered precisely in the parameter regions, where the most exciting physics is observed. One of the major issues, referred to as "misleading convergence", is the tendency of iterative schemes to converge to unphysical fixed points for intermediate-to-strong electronic interactions, regardless of numerical accuracy of the computation. Here, we explicitly verify that the origin of this problem in several established self-consistent many-electron approaches, defined in the general diagrammatic framework of the boson-exchange formalism, resides exclusively in the stability condition of the respective iteration schemes, and not in an intrinsic breakdown of their self-consistent diagrammatic description. This insight enables a simple and general remedy, as recently proposed in Phys. Rev. Lett. 137, 016502 (2026): The redefinition of the iterative procedure, by inverting the unstable eigendirections of the Jacobian associated to the fixed point of the self-consistent algorithm. We illustrate the successful outcome of this procedure by means of systematic calculations performed on testbed, exactly solvable, models. Our results demonstrate that the physical fixed point of the diagrammatic schemes we considered can be stabilized, de facto, across the entire parameter range, including the most challenging nonperturbative/strong-coupling regimes.

</details>


### [100] [Topological band structure of excitons in octochlore KTb$_{3}$F$_{10}$: an analog of the Luttinger Semimetal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11466)
*Olivier Demortier,Françoise Damay,Paul McClarty,Stéphane Raymond,Jacques Ollivier,Sylvain Petit*

TL;DR: 通过高分辨中子散射研究KTb$_3$F$_{10}$（八氯格子的实验实现）中磁激子的色散关系，发现散射强度中的捏点特征，证明其源于立方对称性保护的三带二次能带接触。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁有序体系中通常存在磁长程序和时间反演对称破缺，其能带结构可能比传统非磁体系更丰富。本文旨在探索基态为单态简单乘积的磁系统中色散晶体场激发的能带结构，揭示磁相互作用与能带拓扑（晶体对称性与时间反演对称性）的相互影响。

Method: 以KTb$_3$F$_{10}$（八氯格子octochlore lattice的实验实现）为对象，采用高分辨率中子光谱学测量其玻色型激子（exciton）的色散关系；分析散射强度中的捏点（pinch point）特征，并将主导的长程静磁偶极相互作用与截断到有限壳层的偶极模型进行对比。

Result: 实验观测到散射强度中的捏点特征。系统的主导耦合是长程静磁偶极相互作用，其在区中心的奇异性传递到散射强度中。当偶极作用被截断到有限壳层数时，捏点依然存在，表现为由立方对称性保护的三带二次能带接触（three-band quadratic band touching）。该模型与Luttinger半金属相关。

Conclusion: 该三带二次能带接触模型为捏点散射强度的出现提供了一个普遍的长波长模型，同时涵盖真正的长程偶极模型，丰富了磁性系统中可能存在的能带结构。

Abstract: Understanding the symmetry and topology of band structures in crystalline solids has been among the defining themes of a generation of condensed matter physicists, with implications across many different platforms including electronic bands, photonic crystals, phonons and magnetic excitations. In the latter systems, there is usually magnetic long-range order and time reversal symmetry breaking. Here we further enrich the possible band structures available to magnetic systems by examining dispersive crystal field excitations above a ground state that is a simple product of singlets. In these systems, there is an interplay of magnetic interactions and band topology with both crystal and time reversal symmetries. Using high-resolution neutron spectroscopy we reveal the dispersion relations of bosonic excitons in KTb$_{3}$F$_{10}$, an experimental realization of the octochlore lattice. We show that there are pinch point features in the scattering intensity. The dominant coupling in this system is the long-range magnetostatic dipolar interaction, whose singularity at the zone center is passed onto the scattering intensity. However, the pinch points survive when the range of the dipoles is truncated to finite shell number in the form of a three-band quadratic band touching protected by cubic symmetry. This model, which is related to the Luttinger semi-metal, provides a general long wavelength model for the emergence of pinch point scattering intensity encompassing also the true long-range dipolar model.

</details>

*Satoshi Nishimoto*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用DMRG研究了由铁磁Hund交换耦合的两条t-J链中自旋-1 Haldane对称保护拓扑（SPT）相的空穴掺杂行为。结果表明，在较宽掺杂范围内弦序、有限自旋能隙和Haldane纠缠结构共存，并伴随与单一无能隙电荷模式一致的纠缠熵标度，说明存在具有C1S0低能描述的金属性掺杂Haldane SPT。低掺杂下短程电荷关联降低自旋-1/2缺陷梯级的密度，高掺杂下其位置几乎无关联但自旋被集体纳入关联自旋区。掺杂Haldane SPT终止于完全自旋极化的铁磁相，并在高掺杂处形成重入区。稀薄极限分析将两个铁磁边界分别归因于弱Hund耦合下的自旋-电荷因子化和强Hund耦合下梯级三重态束缚及降低的配对迁移率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在存在巡游载流子时，自旋-1 Haldane对称保护拓扑相的命运及其特征能否在掺杂后保留，并理解其低能物理与相图演化。

Method: 采用密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）计算两条由铁磁Hund交换耦合的t-J链模型，分析弦序、自旋能隙、纠缠结构、纠缠熵标度及电荷关联等，并进行稀薄极限分析。

Result: 在宽掺杂范围内，弦序、有限自旋能隙与Haldane纠缠结构共存，纠缠熵标度与单一无能隙电荷模式一致，支持C1S0低能描述的金属性掺杂Haldane SPT。低掺杂时短程电荷关联抑制自旋-1/2缺陷梯级密度；高掺杂时缺陷位置近乎无关联但其自旋被集体纳入关联自旋区。体系最终进入完全自旋极化铁磁相，并在高掺杂处形成重入区。

Conclusion: 掺杂Haldane SPT在巡游空穴存在下得以保留其标志性特征，但自旋结构被载流子重组；两个铁磁相边界分别源于弱Hund耦合的自旋-电荷因子化和强Hund耦合的梯级三重态束缚导致的配对迁移率降低。

Abstract: We study itinerant hole doping of the spin-$1$ Haldane symmetry-protected topological (SPT) phase in two $t$--$J$ chains coupled by ferromagnetic (FM) Hund exchange. Density-matrix renormalization group calculations show that string order, a finite spin gap, and the Haldane entanglement structure coexist over a broad doping range. Combined with entanglement-entropy scaling consistent with a single gapless charge mode, these results support a metallic doped Haldane SPT with a $C1S0$ low-energy description. At low doping, short-range charge correlations reduce the density of spin-$1/2$ defect rungs, whereas at larger doping their positions become nearly uncorrelated while their spins are collectively incorporated into the correlated spin sector. The doped Haldane SPT terminates in a fully spin-polarized FM phase, which forms a reentrant pocket at high doping in the global phase diagram. Dilute-limit analyses trace the two FM boundaries to spin--charge factorization at weak Hund coupling and to rung-triplet binding with reduced pair mobility at strong Hund coupling. Our results show how mobile carriers reorganize the Haldane spin structure while preserving its characteristic signatures.

</details>


### [102] [Certified reduced-basis emulation of conformal field theories on the fuzzy sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11675)
*Virgil V. Baran*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 结合角动量对角化与认证降阶基代理模型，将模糊球CFT搜索成本降低约两个数量级，并用于定位O(2) Wilson-Fisher CFT临界线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模糊球正则化虽可在数值上研究三维共形场论(CFT)，但需在微观耦合参数空间中反复检验临界性，搜索成本高昂，因此需要大幅降低定位CFT的计算代价。

Method: 采用具有确定总角动量态作为基底的精确对角化，并构建经认证的降阶基(reduced-basis)代理模型，由每个系统尺寸下少量精确解连续重构跨耦合区域的能量、可观测量及其对参数的导数。

Result: 该方法重现了已发表的模糊球Ising模型基态能量分析及三味模糊球O(2) Wilson-Fisher CFT的共形数据(含参数导数定义的量)；在无CFT先验输入下，基态能量判据将临界耦合定位到DMRG值(系统尺寸大一倍以上)的0.5%以内，并映射出耦合平面上的临界线。基态判据能稳健定位临界线，但其沿线选定的点约束较弱，所算维度最接近bootstrap的位置在临界线其他处。

Conclusion: 精确对角化与认证降阶基代理相结合可显著降低模糊球CFT临界性搜索成本(约两个数量级)，在O(2)模型中可高精度定位临界耦合并刻画临界线；但基态判据对临界线上具体点的确定能力有限，维度结果与bootstrap最吻合处不在该判据所选点。

Abstract: Fuzzy-sphere regularization gives numerical access to three-dimensional conformal field theories (CFTs), but locating one requires testing for criticality across the parameter space of microscopic couplings. We reduce the search cost by about two orders of magnitude, combining an exact diagonalization based on states having definite total angular momentum with a certified reduced-basis emulator that reconstructs energies, observables and their derivatives continuously across the couplings from a handful of exact solutions per system size. The combination reproduces the published ground-state-energy analysis of the fuzzy-sphere Ising model and the published conformal data of a three-flavor fuzzy-sphere realization of the O(2) Wilson--Fisher CFT, including quantities defined by parameter derivatives. Applied to the O(2) model with no CFT input, the ground-state-energy criterion locates the critical coupling to within half a percent of a density matrix renormalization group value obtained on systems more than twice the size, and maps the critical line of the model across the coupling plane. Ground-state criteria locate the critical line robustly, but the point they select along it is weakly determined, and the computed dimensions come closest to the bootstrap elsewhere along the line.

</details>


### [103] [Acoustic Wave-Function Imaging of Sublattice Physics in a Kagome Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11678)
*L. Müller,N. Endres,B. Geldiyev,S. Widmann,K. Burkard,M. Ünzelmann,F. Reinert*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在厘米尺度声学kagome晶格中测量完整激发谱，逐格点成像波函数振幅与相位，首次直接实验观测到kagome晶格的子晶格分辨能带结构，包括平带及混合/纯子晶格范霍夫奇异性，结果与最小紧束缚模型吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: kagome能带结构中的Dirac锥、鞍点范霍夫奇异性和平带在三个格点子晶格上具有不同的局域特征，但实验验证这种子晶格特性需要同时获得实空间波函数及其动量空间纹理，难度很大，此前缺乏直接实验证据。

Method: 构建厘米尺度的声学kagome晶格，测量完整激发谱，对每个格点波函数的振幅与相位进行成像，从而获得整个能带的动量依赖子晶格纹理，重点关注平带以及混合子晶格和纯子晶格范霍夫奇异性区域，并与最小紧束缚模型计算进行对比。

Result: 实验测得的子晶格分辨能带纹理与最小紧束缚模型计算高度一致，成功在平带及混合、纯子晶格范霍夫奇异性处分辨出不同的子晶格局域特征。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在kagome晶格中直接实验观测到子晶格分辨的能带结构，证明了声学平台可用于探测经典波系统中的实空间-动量空间联合波函数信息，为研究kagome物理及相关奇异能带效应提供了新途径。

Abstract: The canonical kagome band structure hosts a Dirac cone, saddle-point van Hove singularities (vHS), and a flat band. Characteristically, these features exhibit distinct sublattice localization within the three-site basis of the kagome lattice. However, experimental proof of this sublattice character is challenging and requires direct access to both the real-space wave function and its associated momentum-space texture. Here, we implement an acoustic kagome lattice on the cm scale and measure the full excitation spectrum, thereby imaging the wave function at each lattice site, including both amplitude and phase. This enables investigation of the momentum-dependent sublattice texture across the complete band structure, particularly at the flat band and at the mixed- and pure-sublattice van Hove singularities. Our results are in excellent agreement with minimal tight-binding model calculations and constitutes the first direct experimental observation of the sublattice-resolved band structure in a kagome lattice.

</details>


### [104] [Block entropy area based non-local fermionic mode optimization with gradient disentanglers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11811)
*Miklós Antal Werner,Gero Friesecke,Andor Menczer,Kornél Kapás,Örs Legeza*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于分块熵面积（block entropy area）的模式优化算法，用于优化相互作用费米子多体量子态的矩阵乘积态表示。算法通过全局代价函数的梯度构造长程非相互作用的有效"解纠缠"哈密顿量，并用基于投影分裂的时间依赖变分原理演化时间依赖薛定谔方程以最小化代价函数；与密度矩阵重整化群结合后形成高效低秩迭代基态算法，同时给出优化的单粒子基。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵乘积态（MPS）表示相互作用费米子多体态时，其纠缠结构强烈依赖单粒子模式（轨道）基的选择。此前的模式优化方案多依赖最近邻模式旋转与重排序，优化能力有限，难以有效降低纠缠、提升低秩表示效率。因此需要一个更全局、系统化的纠缠最小化与模式优化框架。

Method: 1) 以分块熵面积作为全局代价函数，刻画MPS的纠缠/键维需求；2) 对代价函数求梯度，构造长程、非相互作用的有效解纠缠哈密顿量；3) 用基于投影分裂（projector splitting）的时间依赖变分原理（TDVP）模拟该哈密顿量驱动的时间依赖薛定谔方程，迭代最小化代价函数；4) 与密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）结合，形成迭代低秩基态求解流程，并同步得到优化的单粒子基。区别于仅用最近邻旋转与重排序的早期方案，本方法允许长程解纠缠变换。

Result: 在二维相互作用费米子格点模型及Fe4S4团簇上进行了验证。结果表明该方法具有鲁棒性，并且在模式优化效果与低秩表示效率方面优于使用最近邻模式旋转和重排序的早期协议。

Conclusion: 基于分块熵面积梯度的长程解纠缠模式优化与DMRG相结合，构成一种高效、鲁棒的低秩迭代基态算法，可同时优化MPS表示的单粒子基。该方法适用于二维费米子格点模型与分子团簇等强关联体系，相较局部（最近邻）模式优化方案具有明显优势。

Abstract: We introduce a systematic block entropy area based mode optimization algorithm for many-body quantum states of interacting fermions represented by matrix product states. From the gradient of a global cost function, the block entropy area, a long-ranged, non-interacting effective disentangler Hamiltonian is formed. We then simulate the time-dependent Schrödinger equation driven by the disentangler Hamiltonian by employing the time-dependent variational principle based on projector splitting, and minimize the cost function. The combination of the density matrix renormalization group with this gradient-based entanglement minimization forms an efficient low-rank iterative ground-state algorithm that also provides an optimized single-particle basis for matrix product state representation. We demonstrate the method on two-dimensional lattice models of interacting fermions and the Fe${_4}$S${_4}$ cluster, and show its robustness and superiority over earlier protocols using nearest-neighbor mode rotations and reorderings.

</details>


### [105] [Unraveling the Kagome Antiferromagnetic $3J$ Model and Its Materials: An Integrated Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11745)
*Xin Lu,Andreas Raikos,Menghan Song,Zezong Li,Lankun Han,Shiliang Li,Sylvain Capponi,Zi Yang Meng,Chengkang Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了面向Dirac量子自旋液体候选材料YCu₃(OH)₆Br₂[Br₁₋ₓ(OH)ₓ]的三耦合Kagome反铁磁Heisenberg模型（3J模型），结合DMRG、NQS与热张量网络方法，确认了介于两个磁有序相之间的中间量子自旋液体相，并给出与实验可比拟的比热特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kagome反铁磁体是寻找量子自旋液体（QSL）的典型平台，而近期实验表明3J材料可能承载Dirac型QSL。然而，微观模型与实验可测量量之间尚缺乏直接、定量的联系，亟需建立从模型到实验指纹的桥梁，以验证QSL候选相并区分其与磁有序相。

Method: 作者对三耦合各向异性Kagome反铁磁Heisenberg模型（3J模型）开展基态与有限温度性质研究。基态方面，采用大规模密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）并结合神经网络量子态（NQS）模拟；有限温度方面，运用指数张量重整化群（XTRG）与切空间张量重整化群（tanTRG）等先进热张量网络方法计算比热。

Result: 研究发现两个磁有序相之间存在一个中间QSL相，且该QSL相与各向同性极限下的Kagome自旋液体基态彼此分离。比热计算表明，在磁有序相中，比热随温度的曲线在约J_{hex}分数倍温度处出现肩峰，而该肩峰在QSL相中消失。这些普适行为与3J材料中有序样品及QSL候选样品的实验比热观测一致。

Conclusion: 本工作将微观模型与实验可测量特征直接联系起来，展示了理解阻挫量子磁体中QSL现象的综合研究范式（参见Meng等，2026），并以3J材料作为代表性案例，为后续研究奠定了基础，同时也为利用比热肩峰的有无来区分磁有序相与QSL相提供了判据。

Abstract: We investigate the ground-state and finite-temperature properties of the kagome antiferromagnetic Heisenberg model with three inequivalent couplings, dubbed the 3J model, which is designed for the candidate Dirac quantum spin liquid (QSL) material YCu$_3$(OH)$_6$Br$_2$[Br$_{1-x}$(OH)$_x$] (see, e.g., Zeng et al., 2024). Employing large-scale density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) supplemented by neural quantum states (NQS) simulations, we identify an intermediate QSL phase between two magnetically ordered phases. We also find that this QSL is separated from the kagome spin liquid ground state at the isotropic limit. To establish a direct comparison with experiments, we compute the specific heat of the model by means of advanced exponential (XTRG) and tangent-space (tanTRG) thermal tensor-network methods. In the magnetically ordered phase, the specific heat over temperature exhibits a shoulder at a temperature that is a fraction of the coupling strength $J_{hex}$, which disappears in the QSL phase. These universal behaviors are consistent with the experimentally observed specific heat in 3J materials for both ordered and QSL candidate samples. Our work thus connects microscopic models with experimentally measurable signatures, exemplifying an integrated approach (see Meng et al., 2026) to understanding QSL phenomena in frustrated quantum magnets, with 3J materials serving as a representative case and providing a foundation for future studies.

</details>


### [106] [Learning Quantum Matter through Attention in Complex Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11792)
*Mingrui Jing,Erdong Huang,Jizhe Lai,Enji Xiong,Jin-Guo Liu,Xin Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作提出面向磁场莫尔连续体中相互作用电子的复数Psiformer神经网络波函数，结合复数隐特征、Hermitian幅值注意力、磁边界条件与费米子反对称性；同等优化步数下其能量低于实数Psiformer，并在多组训练种子与双电子体系上验证了优势，同时给出通量扫描的密度关联与规范不变电流图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁多电子波函数需同时优化幅值与相位，而复数内部表示能否改善变分搜索是神经波函数设计中的实际问题。

Method: 构建Complex Psiformer：采用复数隐特征与Hermitian幅值注意力机制，并引入磁边界条件与费米子反对称性约束；在两个有限超胞中与Real Psiformer进行相同优化步数的对比，并以各自神经Hartree-Fock作为参照；对25-cell体系使用五个训练种子，另单独训练双电子复数态并对照有限组态相互作用参考；通过通量扫描与规范不变电流图分析密度关联与局域环流。

Result: 相同优化步数后，Complex Psiformer在两个有限超胞中能量均低于Real Psiformer，且两者均优于各自神经Hartree-Fock参考；25-cell体系五个种子下复数方案平均优势为1.458 meV/电子且离散度更小；单独训练的双电子复数态与有限组态相互作用参考的能量间隙小于实数对应态；复数态的通量扫描显示非单调密度关联，高磁通下蜂窝状平均密度调制减弱而连通涨落持续存在，规范不变电流图给出局域环流的定性比较。

Conclusion: 这些基准结果支持将该组合架构作为研究有限磁系统能量与电荷分布的变分拟设。

Abstract: Magnetic many-electron wavefunctions require amplitude and phase to be optimized together. Whether a complex internal representation improves this variational search is a practical question for neural wavefunction design. We introduce Complex Psiformer for interacting electrons in a magnetic moiré continuum, combining complex hidden features and Hermitian-magnitude attention with magnetic boundary conditions and fermionic antisymmetry. After the same number of optimization steps, Complex Psiformer reaches lower energies than Real Psiformer in two finite supercells. Both Psiformers also improve on their respective neural Hartree-Fock references. Across five training seeds in the 25-cell system, the mean Complex advantage is 1.458 meV per electron, with a smaller observed spread. A separately trained two-electron Complex state has a smaller energy gap to a finite configuration interaction reference than its Real counterpart. In the Complex states, flux scans show nonmonotonic density correlations and weaker honeycomb mean-density modulation at higher flux, while connected fluctuations persist. Gauge invariant current maps provide a qualitative comparison of local circulation in the optimized states. These benchmarks support the combined architecture as a variational ansatz for studying energies and charge arrangements in finite magnetic systems.

</details>


### [107] [Sub-cycle doublon-holon dynamics in one-dimensional Mott insulators revealed by two-color high-harmonic spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11858)
*Lance Hatch,Aditya Verma,Eric Schultz,Hanjun Yang,Priscila Rosa,Genda Gu,Igor Zaliznyak,Giulio Vampa,Laimei Nie,Hanzhe Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 测量一维Mott绝缘体高次谐波发射的啁啾，揭示了亚光周期尺度上的双占位-空穴（doublon-holon）动力学及其相干性与退相位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态高次谐波光谱正成为探测非平衡多体动力学的工具，但关联材料中强驱动、亚光周期尺度动力学在谐波发射过程中的直接测量仍大多未被探索。

Method: 对原型一维Mott绝缘体的高次谐波发射啁啾进行测量，提取编码在其中的亚光周期双占位-空穴动力学信息，并分析不同谐波级次对应的退相位行为。

Result: 带隙以上谐波呈现正啁啾，表明高次谐波由双占位-空穴复合主导；观察到谐波级次依赖的退相位，可归因于不同谐波对应不同的双占位-空穴行程距离。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了Mott绝缘体中相干的双占位-空穴动力学及其超快退相位，与Floquet工程等其他非平衡光诱导现象相关。

Abstract: Solid-state high-harmonic spectroscopy is becoming an emerging tool for probing nonequilibrium many-body dynamics. Yet, direct measurements of strongly driven, sub-optical-cycle dynamics in correlated materials during high-harmonic emission remain largely unexplored. Here, we measure high-harmonic emission chirp in a prototypical one-dimensional Mott insulator, which encodes strongly driven doublon-holon dynamics at sub-optical-cycle timescales. We observe a positive chirp for above band gap harmonics, indicating that high harmonics are dominated by doublon-holon recombinations. We further show a harmonic order-dependent dephasing, which can be understood through different doublon-holon excursion distances associated with each harmonic. These results reveal coherent doublon-holon dynamics and their ultrafast dephasing in Mott insulators, which is relevant to other nonequilibrium light-induced phenomena, such as Floquet engineering.

</details>


### [108] [Low temperature thermodynamics of $S_{\mathrm{eff}}=1/2$ triangular lattice quantum spin liquid candidate TlYbS$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11881)
*Bishnu P. Belbase,Arjun Unnikrishnan,Piyush Chhallare,Mohan B. Neupane,Eun Sang Choi,Sebastian Erdmann,Muhammad U. Akbar,Mamoun Hemmida,H. -A. Krug von Nidda,Philipp Gegenwart,Arnab Banerjee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过多种测量手段系统研究了三角晶格阻挫磁体TlYbS₂单晶的磁性与热力学性质，揭示了其弱有序态、易面各向异性及丰富的磁场诱导相变行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何阻挫三角晶格反铁磁体中磁有序与量子自旋液体（QSL）之间存在微妙的竞争。Yb基铜铁矿家族AYbX₂为研究这一物理提供了结构纯净的平台，而TlYbS₂作为其中一员，其基态性质尚待明确，尤其是与同族化合物TlYbSe₂和KYbSe₂的对比研究有助于理解自旋子、规范涨落与有序态的关系。

Method: 采用直流磁化率、交流磁化率、电子自旋共振（ESR）和比热测量，从室温至毫开尔文温区对TlYbS₂单晶进行综合表征；并通过单晶X射线衍射确认其结构和Yb³⁺三角层状排布及是否存在位点无序。

Result: 单晶XRD确认TlYbS₂为三角R-3m结构，Yb³⁺三角层分离良好且无可检测位点无序。零场下在约530 mK出现弱热力学异常，可能对应弱有序态。300 mK以下零场磁比热遵循C_m∝T^1.8，接近二次温度依赖，与TlYbSe₂的线性依赖不同。磁化和ESR确立显著的易面各向异性，g_⊥/g_∥≈6.9。约530 mK异常对磁场有强各向异性响应：H⊥c时该异常在约2 T以下仍可见，并在约2.5 T以上连续演化至场诱导有序相，随后出现包括约5至8 T的1/3磁化平台及约17 T完全极化在内的场诱导相序列；H∥c时异常减弱并在约3 T附近被抑制。

Conclusion: TlYbS₂展现出易面各向异性的三角晶格反铁磁行为，其零场弱异常和低温比热幂律与同族化合物存在差异，暗示自旋子与激发规范场相互作用的细节不同。H∥c下异常在约3 T被抑制，与近期报道的从有序态到场诱导QSL的禁闭—退禁闭转变一致，说明TlYbS₂是研究阻挫磁体中QSL与有序态竞争及磁场调控相变的理想平台。

Abstract: Geometrically frustrated triangular-lattice antiferromagnets exhibit a delicate competition between magnetic order and quantum spin liquid (QSL) behavior, with the Yb-based delafossite family $A$Yb$X_2$ providing a structurally clean platform for exploring this physics. Here, we report a comprehensive study of single-crystal TlYbS$_2$ using DC magnetization, AC susceptibility, electron spin resonance (ESR), and specific heat measurements extending from room temperature to the millikelvin regime. Single-crystal X-ray diffraction confirms a trigonal $R\bar{3}m$ structure comprising well-separated triangular layers of Yb$^{3+}$ ions with no detectable site disorder. At zero field, a weak thermodynamic anomaly is observed near $530$ mK, which may indicate the onset of a weakly ordered state similar to that reported in KYbSe$_2$. Below $300$ mK, the zero-field magnetic specific heat follows $C_{\rm m}\propto T^{1.8}$, close to a quadratic temperature dependence, in contrast to the linear temperature dependence reported for the sister compound TlYbSe$_2$, which has been described in terms of the interplay between spinons and thermally excited gauge-flux excitations. Magnetization and ESR measurements establish pronounced easy-plane magnetic anisotropy, with $g_\perp/g_\parallel \approx 6.9$. The anomaly near $530$ mK exhibits a strongly anisotropic response to magnetic field. For $H\perp c$, it remains visible up to approximately $2$ T and continuously evolves toward a field-induced ordered phase above approximately $2.5$ T, followed by a sequence of field-induced phases that includes a $1/3$ magnetization plateau between approximately $5$ and $8$ T and full polarization near $17$ T. In contrast, for $H\parallel c$, the anomaly weakens and is suppressed near $3$ T, consistent with the recently reported confinement--deconfinement transition from an ordered state to a field-induced QSL.

</details>


### [109] [Effective Ionic Valence and Local Magnetic Moment in Kagome Superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11927)
*Ruoshi Jiang,Zi-Jian Lang,Yuzki Oey,Andrea Capa Salinas,Stephen D. Wilson,Yongwei Li,Ilya Shipulin,Yiwen Zhang,Deng Hu,Zhiwei Wang,Hans-Henning Klauss,Zurab Guguchia,Vadim Grinenko,Wei Ku*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过Hartree尺度局域电子结构计算发现kagome超导体AV₃Sb₅与ATi₃Bi₅中V、Ti离子均为2+价、巡游载流子密度相近，差异体现在量子涨落的离子磁矩上；由于几何阻挫难以直接观测，作者引入非磁性Sn杂质解除阻挫，用磁化率和μSR证实局域磁矩存在，并观测到磁化率随杂质浓度系统上升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解kagome超导体家族AV₃Sb₅（A=K、Rb、Cs）与ATi₃Bi₅（A=Rb、Cs）之间反常的相似性及关联行为，需澄清其局域电子结构与载流子来源。

Method: 计算Hartree尺度的局域电子结构以确定离子价态与巡游载流子密度；并系统掺入非磁性Sn杂质局部解除几何阻挫，结合磁化率与μ子自旋旋转/弛豫（μSR）测量验证局域磁矩。

Result: V与Ti离子均为2+价，巡游载流子密度相近，电子数差异体现为量子涨落的离子磁矩；所有实验均发现磁化率随非磁性杂质浓度增加而系统性升高，证实存在明确的局域磁矩。

Conclusion: 所发现的离子磁矩表明需从纯巡游载流子图像转向包含局域离子自旋强关联的范式；相关的原子间与局域-巡游关联为这一新型超导材料家族中丰富的关联行为提供了基础。

Abstract: In order to understand the unexpected similarity and the correlated behavior in kagome superconductor families AV$_3$Sb$_5$ (A = K, Rb, Cs) and ATi$_3$Bi$_5$ (A = Rb, Cs), we investigate the Hartree-scale local electronic structure of these systems. Our result indicates that V and Ti ions are both of 2+ valence such that the corresponding itinerant carrier densities are similar, and the difference in electron count is instead reflected in their quantum fluctuating ionic magnetic moments. However, due to the frustrated lattice geometry of these materials, such local moments are difficult to experimentally observe via standard probes. For verification, we systematically introduce nonmagnetic Sn impurities to locally relieve the geometric frustration and experimentally demonstrate the existence of well-defined local magnetic moments via magnetic susceptibility and muon spin rotation or relaxation ($μ$SR) measurements. All experiments discover a systematic increase of magnetic susceptibility upon increasing nonmagnetic impurity level. Our discovered ionic moments suggest a paradigm shift from the existing itinerant carrier-only picture to one incorporating strong correlation from local ionic spins. The associated interatomic and local-itinerant correlations offer a solid ground for the emergence of the observed rich correlated behavior in this new family of superconducting materials.

</details>
